Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the spin space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 943 and the convergence rate is O(517/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_943(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 943. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^517(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The spin variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 362 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 61 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the spin space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 362 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 61. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The spin operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 779. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 930 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 779 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spin structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 930. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
